# Targeted energy discount: results receipt

Great Britain, FY2027-28. Generated by `uk-energy-reforms run`. Datasets:

- `efrs_1573`: Enhanced FRS 2024-25 (policyengine-uk-data 1.57.3) (policyengine/policyengine-uk-data-private@25af520a, sha256 ef34c1ae2821…). Energy spend imputed from LCFS and raked to NEED 2023 at Ofgem Q2-2026 unit rates. Weights are concentrated (Kish ESS about 1,100) and sum to 30.7m GB households in 2026-27, about 7% above Microcosm (28.6m) and 10% above RF's 28m, so its counts run high.
- `microcosm_national`: Microcosm UK 2024-25 (national release) (policyengine/populace-uk-private@f9d1922c, sha256 aa31bdf67c97…). Energy spend priced with DESNZ QEP FY2024-25, raked to NEED, levelled to Energy Trends. Certified release, published 4 October 2026.

## Headlines

**rf_flat**: RF flat option: 175 GBP per household below 24,000 GBP or passported.
- `microcosm_national`: cost GBP 2.04bn; 11.68m recipients (40.7% of GB households), average GBP 175; passported 26.3%, income test 33.5%; relative AHC poverty -231k people (-66k children).
- `efrs_1573`: cost GBP 2.26bn; 12.90m recipients (41.9% of GB households), average GBP 175; passported 25.9%, income test 36.9%; relative AHC poverty -135k people (-31k children).

## Income cut-offs

Households near each income threshold where support falls, counting only households placed on the schedule by their own income. The offset range holds households above a threshold whose extra income, after tax, is smaller than the support they lose there. Options without an income test have no cut-offs. By region, each option's tables below give the count within GBP 1k above the top threshold and the offset range.

**rf_flat**:

- `microcosm_national`, GBP 24,000: mean drop GBP 175; GBP 1k below 319k (189k lowest-4); GBP 1k above 332k (199k lowest-4, 109k in rel. AHC poverty; ESS 49); offset range 76k (median width GBP 243).
- `efrs_1573`, GBP 24,000: mean drop GBP 175; GBP 1k below 345k (246k lowest-4); GBP 1k above 331k (154k lowest-4, 46k in rel. AHC poverty; ESS 29); offset range 43k (median width GBP 243).
